<h3 class="d3-o-media-object__title">
        Eagles Insider Podcast: Instant reaction from 38-20 loss
      </h3>
<p class="d3-o-media-object__date">
        Oct 13, 2019
      </p>
<div class="d3-o-media-object__summary">
<p>Minnesota’s Kirk Cousins torched the Eagles’ secondary for four touchdowns, the team fell in a 24-3 hole it couldn’t escape and the result was a disappointing loss in Minneapolis on Sunday. Here is the Instant Reaction from the locker room inside U.S. Bank Stadium as the Eagles fell to 3-3 on the season in the first of a three-game road trip.</p>
